# Code simplification review — pass 2

Reviewed baseline: `ecbbd06bd` (`main` at branch creation), branch `lane/code-review-2`, 2026-10-26. **Read-only code review: only this report is changed.** Paths and line numbers refer to that baseline. No server/browser was started; port 3300 was not used.

Navigation: [Conclusions](#1-conclusions-and-accounting) · [Deletion ledger](#3-confirmed-low-risk-deletion-ledger) · [63 named findings/decisions](#4-consolidation-parallel-system-and-smell-findings) · [Crowd/mission map](#5-crowd-retirement-and-mission-ownership-map) · [Largest files](#6-the-40-largest-files-and-concrete-seams) · [Longest functions](#7-the-40-longest-functions) · [Flags/PROD](#8-runtime-flags-prod-hooks-and-static-defaults) · [Batches](#9-batch-plan-and-cross-reference) · [Dead code inventory](#appendix-a-complete-dead-code-scan-disposition) · [All clones](#appendix-b-complete-clone-groups-and-asset-recipe-patterns) · [All flags](#appendix-c-every-static-envquery-read-and-default-expression) · [Smell inventories](#appendix-d-smell-and-single-importer-inventories)

## 1. Conclusions and accounting

**Estimated low-risk removable source: 119 physical lines across 16 evidenced deletions.** This is deliberately narrower than the much larger consolidation opportunity. It excludes unused-export modifiers, generated data, moving functions to other files, tests, asset recipes, conditional crowd retirement, and diagnostic code merely excluded from a build. The exact non-overlapping deletion ledger is below.

The larger simplification is concentrated in shared mission mechanics, duplicated tool harnesses and responsibility boundaries. The rigid-to-skinned rollout is incomplete: **17 manifest entries use `crowdSkin`**. Deleting the entire rigid system now would break remaining figures and validation routes. Source size alone also overstates the problem: the largest file is generated collision metadata.

No finite static review proves the absence of every possible smell. This pass reports all results from the stated scans without a top-N finding cap, gives a disposition for each unused candidate and each clone group, and separates confirmed cleanup from resemblance that does not justify deletion. It does not claim runtime equivalence from static checks.

| Measure | Observed result | Interpretation |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Code inventory | 1,045 src/tools code files; 117,357 physical lines | TS/TSX/JS/MJS/CJS/Python/shell/CSS; excludes JSON and Markdown. More precise than the initial ~95k estimate. |
| Knip 6.43.0, calibrated | 1 unused file; 134 value exports; 62 type exports | Candidates, not 197 deletions. Browser/CLI/ImportType/package entry points require calibration. |
| jscpd 5.4.2 | 2,988 matches; 1,064 file-pair groups; 30-token minimum | 1,128 scanned sources including JSON/Markdown; 27,248 duplicated lines, 18.6483%. Overlap/generated data means this is not removable LOC. |
| Courier builders, additional scope | 35 matches; 421 matched lines; 42.5253% | Two assets/* scripts requested explicitly; savings excluded from src/tools total. |
| TypeScript AST | 11,851 function-like bodies; 0 explicit any; 5,295 ! sites; 2,597 as expressions | Includes callbacks/accessors; ! includes definite-assignment properties; as const is included. |
| Long parameters / branching | 34 TS/JS functions with ≥8 parameters; 176 with control depth≥5 | Complete inventories below. Nested callbacks measured separately; else-if contributes to depth. |
| Compiler unused audit | 16 src/tools diagnostics + 2 out-of-scope test diagnostics | noUnusedLocals/noUnusedParameters are stronger than the normal project configuration. |
| Whole-file clones | No byte-identical source code files in src/tools | Tiny identical asset wrappers exist outside primary scope. |

## 2. Method, reproducibility and limits

Read the repository guidance, specification index, architecture/test contracts and campaign overview; use the relevant E19–E24/E29–E32 mission semantics when consolidating. `specs/`, `folio-2025/`, `references/`, `initial-drafts/`, `experiment/` and reference PNGs were not edited. Tool output and audit scripts were written under `/private/tmp/code-review-2`, not committed. No dependencies or lockfiles were installed into the repository.

Commands actually completed (nonzero lint-style findings exits are expected for Knip/unused checks):

```sh
npx --yes knip --reporter json --no-progress
npx --yes knip --config /private/tmp/code-review-2/knip.json --include-entry-exports --reporter json --no-progress
npx --yes jscpd src tools --absolute --min-tokens 30 --min-lines 1 --reporters json --output /private/tmp/code-review-2/jscpd-absolute
npx --yes jscpd assets/char.courier-male-skin/build.py assets/char.courier-female-skin/build.py --absolute --min-tokens 30 --min-lines 1 --reporters json --output /private/tmp/code-review-2/courier-clones
npx --no-install tsc --noEmit --noUnusedLocals --noUnusedParameters --incremental false
```

Calibration config (entries preserve independently invoked tools instead of declaring all of tools dead):

```json
{
  "entry": [
    "src/main.ts",
    "src/assets/viewer.ts",
    "src/debug/lightingSpike.ts",
    "src/**/*.worker.ts",
    "src/assets/prefetch.worker.ts",
    "tools/**/*.{ts,js,mjs,cjs}",
    "tests/**/*.{ts,js,mjs}",
    "preview/**/*.{ts,js,html}",
    "functions/**/*.ts",
    "*.config.ts",
    "eslint.config.js",
    "scripts/**/*.{ts,js,mjs}"
  ],
  "project": [
    "src/**/*.{ts,tsx,js,mjs}",
    "tools/**/*.{ts,tsx,js,mjs}",
    "tests/**/*.{ts,tsx,js,mjs}",
    "preview/**/*.{ts,js}",
    "functions/**/*.ts",
    "*.config.ts",
    "eslint.config.js",
    "scripts/**/*.{ts,js,mjs}"
  ],
  "includeEntryExports": true
}
```

For future reproducibility pin `knip@6.43.0` and `jscpd@5.4.2`; actual initial invocations used the versions above via npx. Knip default output is retained in Appendix A as a comparison. All tools being entry points intentionally means an unreferenced CLI is not automatically deletable; external/manual invocations need ownership evidence. Exports were checked using TypeScript symbol resolution plus textual searches of src/tools/tests/preview, including variable browser imports. Python functions were parsed with Blender’s Python 3.13 (system 3.9 cannot parse one existing modern f-string); no Blender scene was opened.

Function lengths are inclusive start/end physical lines including nested bodies/comments; nested callbacks also have their own rows and are not additive. Generated files count in the size inventory but not in deletion estimates. jscpd token matches can begin/end in the middle of a physical line; exact line/column spans are retained in Appendix B. Its per-match line counts overlap.

After an initial ENOSPC failure saving its result, a repeated read-only virtual-deletion compiler check used a custom TypeScript CompilerHost to substitute the D01–D15 edits in memory, without modifying source files: **passed, 0 diagnostics (118 proposed deleted physical lines)**. D16 is a type-only forwarding export independently confirmed unused. Normal runtime/browser tests, lint, unit tests and epic verification were not run for this documentation-only review; verification columns specify the checks required when implementing each change.

Preserve side effects: D11 keeps `arc.update(...)`; D15 keeps `windowGlass?.(...)`; D13’s subsequent foot calculation overwrites the scratch vector and constructor already updates world matrices.

## 5. Crowd retirement and mission ownership map

The manifest is the rollout ledger. The following lists are exhaustive at this baseline. Character entries without crowdSkin include hero/afterlife/static-pose assets; absence of the marker does not by itself mean they all pass through CrowdView. In particular, courier skin loading is a separate route.

| --- | --- | --- |
| crowdSkin enabled | 17 | char.corgi, inf.common-worker, inf.nurse, npc.civilian-elderly, npc.firefighter-alive, amb.zebra, inf.zebra, npc.civilian-man-a, npc.civilian-man-b, npc.civilian-woman-a, npc.civilian-woman-b, npc.lab-tech-a, npc.lab-tech-b, npc.lab-guard, npc.depot-clerk, amb.dog, inf.dog |
| character without crowdSkin | 35 | char.survivor-male, char.survivor-female, npc.civilian-adult-m, npc.civilian-adult-f, npc.civilian-kid, npc.patient-zero-courier, npc.brother, npc.mrs-alvarez, npc.police-officer, npc.paramedic, npc.national-guard, npc.survivor-group, npc.helicopter-pilot, char.courier-male, char.courier-female, npc.civilian-elderly.aftermath, npc.paramedic.aftermath, npc.civilian-woman-a.aftermath, npc.survivor-group.aftermath, npc.survivor-group.aftermath.dale, npc.survivor-group.aftermath.sam, npc.survivor-group.aftermath.cap-woman, npc.survivor-group.aftermath.bun-woman, npc.dale.aftermath, npc.rosa.aftermath, npc.mr-okafor.aftermath, npc.mia.aftermath, npc.sam.aftermath, npc.civilian-man-a.aftermath, npc.civilian-man-b.aftermath, npc.civilian-woman-b.aftermath, npc.marcus.aftermath, npc.shelter-survivor.bandaged, npc.shelter-survivor.lying, npc.shelter-survivor.sitting |

| Batch | Deletion prerequisite | Concrete change | Maximum time / risk |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| P01a (repeat per archetype) | Choose one remaining crowd consumer and its derived variants | Migrate manifest/bake, establish posed baseline, remove only superseded outputs with zero references. Assets are outside this report’s LOC total. | 3 h per archetype / medium |
| P01b (repeat per render family) | All live figures in that family use skin, including placeholders/fallbacks | Remove family-specific rigid geometry/source/compaction branch in bakeInfected/CivilianCrowd/CrowdView. Preserve pose atlas math and skin normal/wound logic. | 3 h per family / high |
| P01c (repeat corpse/pick separately) | Corpse pages, detachments and picking use converted weights/owners | Remove rigid-only corpse/pick branches; compare shader and CPU poses. | 3 h each / high |
| P01d | No remaining rigid callers in runtime, preview, tools or tests | Delete legacy-only helpers/types; re-run symbol/import/asset searches and build both render backends. | 3 h / high |
| P03 | Every unconverted derivative now has intended skinned comparison | Remove rigidBaseline manifest/type/validation route, then baseline outputs. Do not remove shared poseTexture/crowdBlendedMatrix. | 2 h / medium |
| P04 (repeat female/male/contact mode) | A/B acceptance and all skin=0 consumers migrated | Remove courier rigid branch and DEFAULT_SKIN switch only after explicit rollout completion. | 3 h per contact mode / high |

Converted archetypes already bypass rigid geometry preparation at CrowdView.ts:211 and CivilianCrowd.ts:141–146; registry.ts:60/74 chooses skeleton cloning. CrowdView.ts:397 still selects skinned matrix, single-joint skin or rigid blend for different geometries. This is per-input specialization, not proof the entire alternative is dead.

## 6. The 40 largest files and concrete seams

G01–G40 are the complete largest-file ranking for the stated code extensions. An extraction is a responsibility improvement, not automatically a deletion. Unless explicitly marked generated/retain, verification is full typecheck plus the affected subsystem’s deterministic/visual checks, risk is medium (high for collision/AI/checkpoint ordering), estimated net removal is 0 until a duplicate is actually replaced. Each proposed seam is its own ≤4 h batch; do not attempt an entire large-file rewrite in one batch.

No environment values or .env contents were read. Fixed/default config is a deletion candidate only when both branches and all callers are known (O02).

| --- | --- | --- |
| __SS__ | main.ts:26 accepts test=1, testApi=1 or debug=true; install at testApi.ts:281 | Compile gate API import in a public-only profile; preserve QA contract and telemetry replay tooling. |
| __SCENE__ | main.ts:29 inside API gate plus scenelab presence; install SceneLab.ts:1741 | Gate scene runtime together with API; keep SceneSession QA build. |
| __MOTIONLAB__ | main.ts:8 motionlab presence independent of DEV; MotionLab.ts:176 | Compile gate isolated lab entry/import, retain QA preview. |
| __ASSET__ | viewer.ts:140 DEV or test presence; preview is a build input | Remove preview entry from lean public build only if deployment policy changes; keep preview dev tools. |
| __LIGHT_SPIKE__ | Optional preview entry under BUILD_SPIKES (vite.config.ts:20) | Already excluded when BUILD_SPIKES unset; truthy string 0 also includes entry, so document boolean semantics. |
| Inspection/Lookdev | debug=true or lookdev URL in main | PO uses debug=true on PROD (telemetry/index.ts:5–7); product decision required before removal. |
| Render probes/profile/id pass | Runtime query guards in Renderer/GameView, not compile-time exclusion | Extract opt-in owner; P11b. Do not strip WebGL compatibility fixes or asset version/build commit constants. |

Safe immediately under the current product contract: D01–D16 source deletions. No debug capability above is unconditionally safe to remove from the shipped QA-capable build. A separate public profile can exclude diagnostic chunks without deleting repository code; no bundle-byte saving is claimed without building that profile.

## 9. Batch plan and cross-reference

Every batch below is ≤4 h including targeted verification. If the slice does not fit, stop after a separately reviewable stage and create another numbered batch. The plan is sequential by dependency; it is not a request to run parallel agents. No code changes are part of this review.

| Batch family | Concrete slice | Time cap | Depends on / completion check |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| D-clean | D01–D16 plus zero-line S06 cleanup | 2 h | Typecheck, calibrated Knip, relevant subsystem smoke; net119-line ledger stays separate from modifiers. |
| O03 | Narrow local-only exports from Appendix A one module family at a time | 2 h each | Preserve ImportType/browser/CLI/package consumers; no counted LOC saving. |
| C01–C06, C09–C16, C20 | One named clone family per batch (not all together) | 3 h each | Apply exact canonical/verification column; keep net estimate honest. C05 includes repeated formation constants. |
| C07a / C07b | Bot threat/input helpers / recovery-medkit-stuck helpers | 3 h each | Keep Walker and level-specific decision trees. |
| C08a / C08b | Capture lifecycle / encoder-output contract | 3 h each | No warm-cache contamination or frame timing change. |
| C17a / C17b / C17c | Courier shared geometry / weights / export, both variants checked each time | 4 h each | Outside primary LOC scope; preserve female/male anatomy and materials. |
| C19 | One proven shared Blender primitive at a time | 3 h each | Authored recipe values remain local. |
| P06a / P06b | Lease/readiness / error handling and cleanup | 3 h each | After C08–C15 avoid counting same scaffold twice; retain cold-load isolation. |
| P07-L1…L10 | One level capture/restore seam | 4 h each | State roundtrip and subsequent ticks; explicit mission event order. |
| P09a / P09b | Mission interaction descriptor / presentation adoption | 3 h each | Rings and actual input predicates agree before removing old render branches. |
| P11a / P11b | QA/public build capability / render-probe extraction | 4 h each | Requires deployment/product decision; both builds work, preview remains usable. |
| P12 | Profiler lifecycle reinstall/dispose | 3 h | Replace level then profile, verify current systems/listener cleanup. |
| O01 / O02 / O04 / O06 | One specific contract/default decision per batch | 2 h each | Do not remove test-only branch before updating intended contract. |
| S01 / S02 / S03 / S04 | One world boundary / typed adapter / API / dispatcher at a time | 3 h each | No universal framework; maintain allocations and deterministic order. |
| S07 | Legacy occlusion helper and tests migration | 3 h | Keep live lensPoint/hugDistance; verify current visibility path. |
| G01…G40 / F01…F40 | One concrete seam from largest-file/function table | 4 h per seam | Extract/review before further split. Generated/data rows are retention decisions. No extra LOC credit. |
| P01a…P01d / P03 / P04 | Per-archetype/family migration and conditional deletion | 2–3 h per slice | Detailed prerequisites in §5; do not interpret total rollout as a single 4 h task. |

## Appendix B. Complete clone groups and asset recipe patterns

Each B-group is an unordered file pair; all detector matches for that pair are retained with start/end line:column spans, token count and jscpd line count. Same-file groups name the earlier block as the canonical seed. Named C-findings override the default seed. Unselected code groups are resemblance candidates: a C reference means a related reviewed family, not that every short match is covered by that extraction. Keeper is a nominated extraction seed, not permission to delete the other side; estimated removable0, medium risk, verify semantics and callers before extracting. Generated/authored data groups retain both with 0 deletion: change the generator/default factory only where C16 establishes identical policy. JSON schemas/fixtures are not hand-merged because they share keys. This keeps all 30-token results without falsely treating short syntax matches as defects.

## Appendix C. Every static env/query read and default expression

Exact source expressions are the default specification. ?? / || / ternaries show fallback; has means false when absent; a bare get/env read is unset/null until its caller supplies a value. Repeated names have per-call defaults. Assignment-only occurrences are identified as writes, not off/on controls. This catalogs source reads, not observed deployment values. All rows: retain pending explicit retirement,0 estimated lines, medium risk if removed; verify each caller’s invocation and fallback.

| Kind / name | Location | Exact default / conditional expression |
| --- | --- | --- |
| compile DEV | [src/assets/viewer.ts:50](../../../src/assets/viewer.ts#L50) | <code>manifest: (import.meta.env.DEV &#124;&#124; params.has('test')) && params.has('production')&lt;br&gt;      ? (manifest as AssetDef[]).map(def =&gt; def.id === id ? { ...def, status: 'integrated', ...(params.has('inspection') ? { requiredNodes: [], animatedNodes: [], sockets: [], dimensions: { ...def.dimensions, tolerance: Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY } } : {}) } : def) : undefined</code> |

BUILD_SPIKES is outside src/tools in vite.config.ts:20 (unset/off, any truthy string enables optional entry). __BUILD_COMMIT__ and __ASSET_VERSIONS__ are build constants, not diagnostic globals. No claim is made that environment knobs unused in checked-in commands are never set by CI/operator shells.
